  swap/restore consumer at :1040/:1053 — process_file_signature_sha256
  :1065 EVP chain, capture ladder :1169-1207, print/iconv cluster
  :1226-1362 + get_UTF8_fname :85): the fflows probe never crosses
  them; their bodies + the fflows call sites at file_flows.cc:473/
  :486/:531 land together in row 17r, so the semantics are never split
  across rows.
- C layout constants (A_SZ_FF=216, SZ_SLOT=16, member offsets) are
  pinned literals: the Rc/Vec port shape does not reproduce a sizeof
  (fcache SIZEOF_FILE_NODE precedent).


## file_api/file_lib heavy FileContext half + file_segment/file_mempool/circular_buffer (row 17r)

Harness reality:

- Fresh `tools/fctx_harness/` copied from the fflows donor; measured witness
  delta ZERO new symbols (fctx_harness/PLAN.md §3). The gate link carries
  THREE UT TUs — [file_flows] + [file_cache] carried, [file_mempool] NEW =
  18 cases / 91 assertions — under the duplicate-symbol rule: every
  UT-compiled TU's plain .o is excluded from the gate link (nm dup errors
  otherwise; RECON-verified this row). `file_lib.cc`'s lone UNIT_TEST block
  (:1363 [file_info], 19 cases, remains filib's gate) is excised with a
  rfind + brace-balance guard on BOTH builds so those cases never leak into
  fctx's gate.txt; the early :37/:54 `#ifdef UNIT_TEST` fragments stay.
- ZERO upstream Catch cases live in the :825–1362 heavy span: ground truth
  = probe vectors (610 lines, md5 839b5566be0a2191876e0f34b4ed56e4, byte-identical
  across clean rebuilds incl. the docs-time re-verification) + the 17o G-block
  pins. The fflows donor's committed vectors stay untouched (retire rule).
- The heavy-half bodies and the `file_flows.cc:473/:486/:531` call sites land
  in the SAME commit (semantics never split across rows): the 17q pinned
  panics are deleted together with their unlock, so no intermediate build
  carries both a live body and a panic.

Structural deviations (behavior preserved):

- **EVP → hand-rolled SHA-256** (`src/helpers/sha256.rs`, FIPS 180-4, zero
  external deps): the crate has no OpenSSL dependency and the host has no dev
  packages, so `EVP_MD_CTX_new/Init_ex/DigestUpdate/copy_ex/DigestFinal/free`
  map to `Sha256Ctx::{new,init,update,snap,finalize}` / drop (call table in the
  module header). `Clone` IS `EVP_MD_CTX_copy_ex` (the FLUSH arms :1093-1105/
  :1117-1129 snapshot the running state; the live ctx stays untouched — pinned
  by M2_H1 vs M2_HREF). The C `copy_ex`/`!= 1` failure arms are compile-visible
  dead branches upstream never fires; kept dead, not sed away. The C probe ran
  REAL libcrypto (node-headers recipe) and the Rust digests match it
  byte-for-byte (M1_HASH 'abc' = BA78... printed as 2-byte UPPERCASE pairs,
  file_lib.cc:1280).
- **Mixed-allocator sha buffer** (file_lib.cc:1096/:1120 `snort_alloc` vs
  :1139/:1155 `new uint8_t[]`, freed by `snort_free` :737 vs `reset_sha`'s
  `delete[]` :362-366 — a genuine UB pair in C where no read distinguishes
  the allocators): the Rust port keeps ONE owned `Option<Vec<u8>>`;
  `finish_signature_lookup`'s else-branch nulling (C :736-739) lands as
  `Option::take` — the observable `P1_RESETSHA null_after=1` is pinned, the
  mismatched free itself is structurally excluded and never replicated.
- `std::recursive_mutex user_file_data_mutex` (lock_guard sites :1068/:1172/
  :1191) is not reproduced: Rust exclusivity stands in (row 17p precedent);
  the lock/unlock ORDER is only modeled where observable.
- **FileCapture stays a trait seam**: C `process_file_capture` :1169-1187
  allocates a real `new FileCapture(min,max)` riding the file-static
  `file_mempool` (file_capture.cc:55) + CircularBuffer + the `std::thread`
  writer; the port injects `Box<dyn FileCaptureApi>` (`new_capture` factory,
  file_lib.rs). The capture state ladder (N-block) is pinned through the seam
  witnesses; the real TU (913+150, 4 [file_capture] cases incl. the async-
  writer dance) is row 17s proper. The C dtor chain
  (`stop_file_capture` -> `~FileCapture` ordering, 17q-pinned) is unchanged.
- `FileSegments`: C's doubly-linked `next/prev` list (always offset-sorted)
  -> `Vec<FileSegment>` in list order; every cursor (`left`, the closer-end
  `le` search, the overlap `left = left->next` ladder with the
  `*left = (*left)->next` caller-slot re-aim) is the same walk over
  `Option<usize>` indices, so insertion order and FILE_DEBUG print order land
  exactly as C. `offset` keeps the C uint32_t field width (add/process args
  are uint64 and truncate at node creation, literally). The
  `process_one` ↔ `FileContext::process` (position form) mutual recursion is
  threaded as explicit args + the free `process_pos` entry — no
  self-referential struct. `clear()` empties the Vec but the owning slot
  stays Some (O5: queued_ptr=1 after the false-arm clear — the C
  `file_segments` pointer likewise survives clear; nulling only via
  `remove_segments` / dtor).
- `file_segments: Option<()>` -> `Option<Rc<RefCell<FileSegments>>>`; the dtor
  `delete file_segments` arm is the handle drop.
  `file_signature_context: Option<()>` -> `Option<Sha256Ctx>` (dtor's
  `EVP_MD_CTX_free` arm = drop-with-struct, guard meaning preserved).
  `file_type_context: Option<()>` (C `void*`, file_lib.h:231) STAYS the seam:
  no pinned path reads it — only the file-type inspector plumbs it, a later row.
- `IpsContext` writes (file_lib.cc:1021-1027) materialize as `PacketView`
  fields (file_cache.rs): `file_data: Option<Vec<u8>>` models the non-null
  `std::string_view` (fdp=1 pin, len re-materialized at the read), plus
  `proto_bits` (PROTO_BIT__PDU |= reads back 64 in L2_PLAIN), `file_pos`,
  `file_type_process`. The view folds packet + IpsContext into one value.
- C layout constants (`A_SZ ctx=424 segs=32 slot=16`) are pinned literals:
  the Rc/Vec port shape does not reproduce a sizeof (fcache FileNode precedent).
- The fcache/fflows parity fixtures gained the new `PacketView` fields as
  zero-init defaults; their replays never drive the :1021-1027 arms, so the
  17p/17q committed transcripts and expectations are untouched.
- THREAD_LOCAL / file_counts / FilePolicyHandle / two-FileConfig-view
  disciplines carry over unchanged from rows 17p/17q.

## file_api/file_capture.{h,cc} (row 17s)

Harness reality:

- Fresh `tools/fcap_harness/` (fctx donor, retire rule): probe TU is the
  `-DUNIT_TEST` copy keeping every early hook fragment (:45/:65/:100/:112/
  :197/:203) + the UT scaffold :620-:807 with ONLY the 4 TEST_CASE bodies
  (:808-:912) excised; the gate runs those 4 cases verbatim. C transcripts =
  `vectors.txt` (27 lines, md5 0208a69116861a506f0c46a7dc36b2ac, NEVER
  edited); witness delta ZERO new stubs (PLAN §3).
- The 4 upstream cases ARE multi-threaded (1 writer + 2 flush callers);
  the probe/gate determinism = the AsyncWriterGate hook ladder, and the
  Rust port replays THAT ladder single-threaded (below).

Structural deviations (behavior preserved):

- **Single-thread async model** (row 17r `user_file_data_mutex` precedent;
  fcap PLAN §8-2): `capture_mutex`/`capture_cv` (:58-:59) and
  `std::thread* file_storer` (:60; spawned :171, joined :188) are
  comment-transcribed, NEVER ported. `writer_thread` (:79-:117) splits at
  the UT hook sites (:100-:103 / :112-:115) into `writer_pop` +
  `writer_finish` so the gate-ladder interleavings (pop-before-store,
  store-before-release, nonempty-idle predicate window — vectors F/G) stay
  scriptable; `flush()` (:194-:209) inline-performs the writer's
  iterations (the C predicate :207 waits ON the writer); `exit()`
  (:178-:192) models join as "drain the queue first" per the C :89-:91
  comment. A flush over a parked pop PANICS loudly where C would wait on
  the hook (PLAN §9-1 join face made observable) — pinned by the parity
  test's scripted release order (:794-:796).
- **C file-statics live in `FileCaptureShared`** shared by every capture
  (17r in-object precedent + 17m FileStatsEnv discipline): `file_mempool`,
  `capture_block_size`, `files_waiting`, `file_storing`, `running`, plus a
  `file_storer_alive` flag for the :60 thread pointer.
- **store_file_async consumes `Box<Self>`** (:564-:584): C pushes raw
  `this` and the writer `delete`s it (:106) — the Rust queue OWNS the Box
  (move = the ownership transfer). The trait seam
  `Box<dyn FileCaptureApi>` never reaches the async half: the C queue
  feeders are `file_policy.cc:195-197` (a later row) and the UT
  `enqueue` (concrete). A queued capture's `Rc` back-pointer to the
  shared statics mirrors C's leak-until-drained window.
- **THREAD_LOCAL `file_counts` merged**: C's `delete file` (:106) bumped
  the WRITER thread's TLS, invisible on main (probe pin
  `F_tls_split main_files_released_d=0`, vectors.txt:22). Single-thread
  port = ONE counts object; which counter bumps where is unchanged, only
  the per-thread split merges (delta becomes visible 1). Asserted in
  `tests/fcapture_parity_test.rs` (case-C mirror).
- **Layout constants pinned, never `size_of`** (fcap PLAN §9-3):
  `SIZEOF_FILE_CAPTURE=72` (:220/:591 slot math) and
  `SIZEOF_FILE_CAPTURE_BLOCK=16` (:294/:318/:335/:481 payload offset) +
  `SLOT_BLOCK64=136` cross-check — probe CONST line only. The block
  keeps its mempool slot id for m_release/m_free and an owned
  `payload: Vec<u8>` (capacity = `capture_block_size`; C payload region =
  slot + 16, the 56-byte tail never written in C — structurally
  excluded).
- Block chain `FileCaptureBlock* next` -> `Option<usize>` arena index;
  `current_data` (:141) -> owned copy (the C dangling-pointer hazard is
  structural in Rust); `current_data_len > current_data.len()` C UB is
  unreachable by construction.
- `fopen(name, "wx")` (:536) -> `OpenOptions::create_new(true)`; the
  nameless-capture fast-fail (:537-:540, vectors `B_store_nameless_noop`
  + gate disk-silence) lands as the silent `Err` arm. `write_file_data`
  (:495-:526) keeps the 3-retry do/while literally; `ferror`'s codes map
  to the failing call's raw errno (C stdio ferror is 0/1 — the EINTR/
  EAGAIN arms never fired in C either; pinned deviation).
- `get_instance_file` (:577) seam's DEFAULT is the harness witness
  (stubs.cc:182 `s = n` — vectors H `name_eq_sha=1` pin THAT); the real
  thread.cc:129 formatter needs the NamingConfig/ThreadState/LogEngine
  trio, injectable via `FileCaptureShared::set_instance_file` (the
  parity disk test wires an absolute temp dir: threads + cargo rule out
  the probe's process-wide chdir hygiene trick).
- `collect_mem_usage` drops the unused-in-body `Builder&` receiver per
  the fstats FileStatsHooks precedent (the C :595-:599 calls are the
  builder's statics, `this` never touched); the five counters + their
  names/values are vector-C verbatim.
- TestAccess scaffold semantics (resettable/reset/enqueue/
  released_buffers, :625-:659) land as pub methods (fcap PLAN §8-2);
  `released_buffers` on a null pool answers 0 where C derefs (pinned
  deviation, fixture-reset sequence only).
- Seam closure: `FileEnvLite::default().capture_factory` is now
  `RealFileCaptureFactory` (file_lib.rs:1115-:1125) — the C `new` never
  answers null, so the 17r pinned-absent `.expect` holds by
  construction; `NoFileCaptureFactory` stays for witness opt-out (the
  17r HubCapture fixtures stay green untouched — vectors unchanged).

  caller (drop == the probe's delete[] + delete, leak_delta 0).

  (file_events.h:99/:124-:125); the Rust mirror keeps padding
  implicit-zero (file_cache.rs make_key `{}` discipline) — wire

- handle's cc:35 `FileInfo file = fe.get_file_ctx()` copy arrives at

## file_api.{h,cc} umbrella TU (row 17v)

  row's "umbrella/factory TU" label is WRONG at the code level —

  "No static, no global, no factory." The header is the shared
  vocabulary umbrella; the "factory" reading would invent symbols.
- Canonical-home consolidation (17u recipe; vectors.md5
  a953a5017b74f5d44dbba14df48ac5ea pins every wire value). New home
  `src/file_api/file_api.rs`; OLD sites -> `pub use` re-points so all
  26 parity suites keep their imports:
  * file_lib.rs: 6 enum const-blocks (FileVerdict/FilePosition/
    FileCaptureState/FileSigState/FileDirection/FileCharEncoding),
    UTF_16_LE_BOM_LEN, FileState, get_character_encoding,
    UserFileDataBase trait -> re-point to file_api.rs.
  * file_cache.rs: FileAction trio (:308-:310), FileCacheClearReport
    (:116-:121), FilePolicyApi trait (:545-:556), FilePolicyHandle +
    PolicyStore (:558-:612) -> re-point to file_api.rs.
  * LEAVE-IN-PLACE (explicit ruling, not oversights): FILE_ID_MAX stays
    at file_identifier.rs:21 (+file_stats.rs:22 mirror) — fid row turf,
    carried 17u ruling; FileTypeBitSet gets NO Rust wrapper type (word
    form `&mut [u64; FILE_ID_MAX/64]` stands; C `bitset=128` pinned as
    a vector, unmatchable by a Rust wrapper anyway).
- Dead feature macros h:37-:41 (ENABLE_FILE_TYPE_IDENTIFICATION /
  ENABLE_FILE_SIGNATURE_SHA256 / ENABLE_FILE_CAPTURE / FILE_ALL_ON /
  FILE_ALL_OFF): ported as wire-pinned u32 consts (M_flags
  `1,2,4 allon=ffffffff alloff=0`) but DELIBERATELY NOT wired to the
  FileService enable statics — grep-0 consumers src-wide at fe29cbe
  (fcapi PLAN 9-R5); connecting dead masks to the live enable path is
  a semantic invention the vectors cannot catch. FileProcessType
  (h:91-:96) likewise grep-0-dead: consts 0/1/2 only, no behavior.
- UserFileDataBase copy-ctor (h:137), copy-assign (h:138) and private
  copy() (h:141) stay UNPORTED — LEFT-OPEN with grep evidence: zero
  definitions anywhere in the C tree (`grep -rn 'UserFileDataBase::'
  src` -> 0 at fe29cbe, re-run this session); any C TU copying one is
  a LINK ERROR, so a Rust equivalent would fabricate behavior the
  linker itself forbids (PLAN 9-R4 dead-body trap). Production only
  ever news/deletes (file_cache.cc:1176, set/get file_lib.cc:541/:554).
  The 17o opaque trait shape rode the move unchanged.
- sizeof posture (probe C_sz; NEVER fake layouts, 17u precedent):
  `fpolbase=16` (vptr 8 + std::atomic_uint 4 + tail pad) and `ufd=8`
  (vptr-only) are C++-object facts with no Rust counterpart (trait
  object = fat ptr, no vptr member) — pinned in the vectors + this
  doc, NOT asserted via size_of. `size_of::<FileState>()==8`
  (`fstate=8`) and `size_of::<FileCacheClearReport>()==16` (`fccr=16`)

## codecs/ip/checksum.h (row 18b — the 284-L all-inline checksum header lib)

- Union punning NOT modeled with Rust unions (R5 ruling): checksum.h:30-:42
  / :44-:56 declare `union Pseudoheader { struct {...} hdr; uint16_t
  arr[6]; }` (+ the :54 arr[18] form). The port keeps the struct as the
  type and reproduces the `arr` word view via explicit `arr_words()` /
  `as_bytes()` byte-layout fns (sip@0 dip@4 zero@8 proto@9 len@10 /
  sip[4]@0 dip[4]@16 zero@32 proto@33 len@34 — the LE compiler layout,
  zero padding, sizes 12/36 re-asserted Rust-side as const _: () =
  assert!(...) mirroring the :41/:55 static_asserts). The ONLY observable
  through the union in C code paths is the word walk (add_ipv{4,6}_
  pseudoheader reads ph.arr — checksum.h:113/:126), which `arr_words()`
  answers byte-exactly: PH4 word dump `007f 0100 007f 0100 0600 1400`
  and PH6 `arr17=0x1400` replay in vectors:15/:16.
- Endianness posture pinned to the ground host (deviation-from-portable,
  not from C): read_word (:83-:88 memcpy) compiles to a LITTLE-endian
  u16 load on x86_64; the port's `u16::from_le_bytes` is value-identical
  there. On a hypothetical BE host the port would flip while C would too
  (same memcpy) — the vectors themselves embed LE words (K_cksum15
  0xbb44 is an LE fact: the odd byte 0xdd joins as 0xdd00 in the final
  sum), so parity is pinned to the pinned-host family; the later ip-codec
  rows inherit this posture (csum PLAN §1).
- The C OVERLOAD SET cannot be named in Rust: icmp_cksum(buf,len,ph6) vs
  icmp_cksum(buf,len) (:208/:218) and tcp/udp x (Pseudoheader,
  Pseudoheader6) (:223/:235, :247/:259) land as icmp_cksum/_v6 +
  tcp_cksum4/6 + udp_cksum4/6 (6 C entry points -> 6 Rust names; the
  decl block :60-:66 is the overload census). Overload-resolution
  semantics themselves have no Rust counterpart — the mapping is 1:1
  arity+type, so no arm ambiguity can drift.
- The by-reference in/out triple of detail::add_{tcp,udp,ip}_header
  (`const uint8_t*& d, size_t& len, uint32_t& cksum` — :153-:155 etc.)
  rides an explicit (slice, len, &mut cksum) -> (advanced slice, new len)
  return: the CALLERS (:230-:232, :242-:244, :254-:256, :266-:268,
  :275-:277) re-thread both outputs exactly as C's reference parameters
  do; the guard bail returns the INPUT slice unadvanced + len 0, which is
  the vectors' ptr_moved=0 face (probe.cc:9 `d != short_buf`) — third
  fact beyond the C suite's CHECK_EQUAL(0u, len) (vectors:1-3, Q3; the
  csum_guard_arms_keeps_cksum_and_cursor test pins the accumulator-
  untouched face with an armed non-zero cksum, a posture the
  transcript's cks=0x0000 face cannot distinguish on its own).
- (ptr,len) window semantics: C walks the CALLER-DECLARED length over a
  raw pointer; the Rust port takes `&[u8]` + the declared `len` and
  slices `&buf[..len]` — declared-len-shorter-than-slice is the suite
  fixture (short_buf[16] walked at 4/15/1/0), declared-len-LONGER is a C
  OOB read = a Rust index panic (same never-exercised posture as the
  17t wire-walk ruling; the C suite/probe never does it).
- uint32 accumulation uses wrapping_add on the cksum chain (C's mod-2^32
  unsigned arithmetic): the walk itself cannot overflow (<= 60 x 0xffff),
  but the transcription rule (skill: every C unsigned add ports as
  wrapping) holds so a future longer-buffer caller cannot trip a debug
  panic where C silently wrapped.
- The :59 file-scope `static uint16_t cksum_add(...)` FORWARD DECL + the
  :280 inline definition pair has NO Rust counterpart (no name-lookup
  order in Rust); the sole trace is the -O0 nm witness `t
  _ZN8checksumL9cksum_addEPKvm` (csum build.sh:92 grep) — the Rust
  `cksum_add` mirrors the :280-:281 definition only (Q6 ruling).
- CppUTest suite Rust-side (R1/E4, the 16g/16h precedent): NO native C
  gate exists for this row — the CppUTest headers are absent on the host
  (build.sh E4 wall receipt re-proven EVERY build: 'CppUTest/
  CommandLineTestRunner.h: No such file') and the suite's main() (:198-
  :201) is runner framework, not material. The 3 TEST_GROUPs (:56/:107/
  :156, all empty `{ };` fixtures -> each #[test] re-creates clean state,
  skill rule 10) x 10 TESTs translate 1:1 as up_gate_* fns. The FOUR
  CHECK(true) arms (:122/:130/:143/:149) pin ONLY "the call returned"
  (the hardening regression proof — pre-hardening the underflow walk
  SIGSEGV'd, never reached the CHECK; suite prose :20-:26); their exact
  return values are probe-pinned and cited (vectors:5-8), never asserted
  blind from CppUTest semantics (unverifiable-fixture rule).
- add_udp_header statement order (len-= at :185 BEFORE d+= at :186,
  reversed vs tcp :167/:168 / ip :203/:204): independent locals, no
  observable difference; kept as a cite note at the Rust fn, statement
  order otherwise literal.
- The 2026 hardening REGRESSION itself is a quirk pin, not a deviation:
  the guard arms (:157-:162/:175-:180/:193-:198) are the CURRENT ground
  behavior (shallow clone: pre-hardening code unrecoverable, suite header
  prose :20-:26 is the record); the port transcribes the guarded form
  ONLY and NEVER the old `len -= N` underflow (NEXT.md:278 ruling).
- Fixture note (not a deviation): the C suite fills ph.hdr fields
  individually (:113-:118 etc.) — the Rust struct literals set every
  field at once (Default for the `ph6{}` zero-init test :127 shape);
  identical 12/36-byte storage, field order identical.
- src/codecs/ layout: NEW dir + mod.rs with the single `pub mod checksum`
  (C `codecs/ip/checksum.h` lands FLAT as src/codecs/checksum.rs — the
  flat-vs-dir convention call is deferred to 18c when dirs multiply,
  csum PLAN §7); lib.rs gains `pub mod codecs;` in the existing
  group ordering (dir-name sort slot before file_api).
